{"forms":[{"form":"waslas","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"wasla","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"waṣla","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A diacritic added to the Arabic letter alif ا to form ٱ, also called \"hamzatu l-waṣli\" - هَمْزَةُ الْوَصْلِ (hamzatu l-waṣli) or \"alifu l-waṣli\" - أَلِفُ الْوَصْلِ (ʔalifu l-waṣli).
